Index your markdown notes, meeting transcripts, documentation, and knowledge bases. Search with keywords or natural language. Ideal for your agentic flows.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eQMD combines BM25 full-text search, vector semantic search, and LLM re-ranking—all running locally via node-llama-cpp with GGUF models.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ca target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" href=\"/tobi/qmd/blob/main/assets/qmd-architecture.png\"\u003e\u003cimg progress in the \u003ca href=\"/tobi/qmd/blob/main/CHANGELOG.md\"\u003eCHANGELOG\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"# Install globally (Node or Bun)\nnpm install -g @tobilu/qmd\n# or\nbun install -g @tobilu/qmd\n\n# Or run directly\nnpx @tobilu/qmd ...\nbunx @tobilu/qmd ...\n\n# Create collections for your notes, docs, and meeting transcripts\nqmd collection add ~/notes --name notes\nqmd collection add ~/Documents/meetings --name meetings\nqmd collection add ~/work/docs --name docs\n\n# Add context to help with search results, each piece of context will be returned when matching sub documents are returned. This works as a tree. This is the key feature of QMD as it allows LLMs to make much better contextual choices when selecting documents. Don't sleep on it!\nqmd context add qmd://notes \u0026quot;Personal notes and ideas\u0026quot;\nqmd context add qmd://meetings \u0026quot;Meeting transcripts and notes\u0026quot;\nqmd context add qmd://docs \u0026quot;Work documentation\u0026quot;\n\n# Generate embeddings for semantic search\nqmd embed\n\n# Search across everything\nqmd search \u0026quot;project timeline\u0026quot;           # Fast keyword search\nqmd vsearch \u0026quot;how to deploy\u0026quot;             # Semantic search\nqmd query \u0026quot;quarterly planning process\u0026quot;  # Hybrid + reranking (best quality)\n\n# Get a specific document\nqmd get \u0026quot;meetings/2024-01-15.md\u0026quot;\n\n# Get a document by docid (shown in search results)\nqmd get \u0026quot;#abc123\u0026quot;\n\n# Get multiple documents by glob pattern\nqmd multi-get \u0026quot;journals/2025-05*.md\u0026quot;\n\n# Search within a specific collection\nqmd search \u0026quot;API\u0026quot; -c notes\n\n# Export all matches for an agent\nqmd search \u0026quot;API\u0026quot; --all --files --min-score 0.3\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Install globally (Node or Bun)\u003c/span\u003e\nnpm install -g @tobilu/qmd\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e or\u003c/span\u003e\nbun install -g @tobilu/qmd\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Or run directly\u003c/span\u003e\nnpx @tobilu/qmd ...\nbunx @tobilu/qmd ...\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Create collections for your notes, docs, and meeting transcripts\u003c/span\u003e\nqmd collection add \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e~\u003c/span\u003e/notes --name notes\nqmd collection add \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e~\u003c/span\u003e/Documents/meetings --name meetings\nqmd collection add \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e~\u003c/span\u003e/work/docs --name docs\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Add context to help with search results, each piece of context will be returned when matching sub documents are returned. This works as a tree. This is the key feature of QMD as it allows LLMs to make much better contextual choices when selecting documents. Don't sleep on it!\u003c/span\u003e\nqmd context add qmd://notes \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003ePersonal notes and ideas\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\nqmd context add qmd://meetings \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003eMeeting transcripts and notes\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\nqmd context add qmd://docs \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003eWork documentation\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Generate embeddings for semantic search\u003c/span\u003e\nqmd embed\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Search across everything\u003c/span\u003e\nqmd search \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003eproject timeline\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e           \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Fast keyword search\u003c/span\u003e\nqmd vsearch \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003ehow to deploy\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e             \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Semantic search\u003c/span\u003e\nqmd query \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003equarterly planning process\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e  \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Hybrid + reranking (best quality)\u003c/span\u003e\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Get a specific document\u003c/span\u003e\nqmd get \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003emeetings/2024-01-15.md\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Get a document by docid (shown in search results)\u003c/span\u003e\nqmd get \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e#abc123\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Get multiple documents by glob pattern\u003c/span\u003e\nqmd multi-get \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003ejournals/2025-05*.md\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Search within a specific collection\u003c/span\u003e\nqmd search \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003eAPI\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e -c notes\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Export all matches for an agent\u003c/span\u003e\nqmd search \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003eAPI\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e --all \u003ccode\u003e--json\u003c/code\u003e and \u003ccode\u003e--files\u003c/code\u003e output formats are designed for agentic workflows:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"# Get structured results for an LLM\nqmd search \u0026quot;authentication\u0026quot; --json -n 10\n\n# List all relevant files above a threshold\nqmd query \u0026quot;error handling\u0026quot; --all --files --min-score 0.4\n\n# Retrieve full document content\nqmd get \u0026quot;docs/api-reference.md\u0026quot; --full\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Get structured results for an LLM\u003c/span\u003e\nqmd search \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003eauthentication\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e --json -n 10\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e List all relevant files above a threshold\u003c/span\u003e\nqmd query \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003eerror handling\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e --all --files --min-score 0.4\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Retrieve full document content\u003c/span\u003e\nqmd get \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003edocs/api-reference.md\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e --full\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eMCP Server\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-mcp-server\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: MCP Server\" href=\"#mcp-server\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eAlthough the tool works perfectly fine when you just tell your agent to use it on the command line, it also exposes an MCP (Model Context Protocol) server for tighter integration.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eTools exposed:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003equery\u003c/code\u003e — Search with typed sub-queries (\u003ccode\u003elex\u003c/code\u003e/\u003ccode\u003evec\u003c/code\u003e/\u003ccode\u003ehyde\u003c/code\u003e), combined via RRF + reranking\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003eget\u003c/code\u003e — Retrieve a document by path or docid (with fuzzy matching suggestions)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003emulti_get\u003c/code\u003e — Batch retrieve by glob pattern, comma-separated list, or docids\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003estatus\u003c/code\u003e — Index health and collection info\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eClaude Desktop configuration\u003c/strong\u003e (\u003ccode\u003e~/Library/Application Support/Claude/claude_desktop_config.json\u003c/code\u003e):\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-json notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"{\n  \u0026quot;mcpServers\u0026quot;: {\n    \u0026quot;qmd\u0026quot;: {\n      \u0026quot;command\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;qmd\u0026quot;,\n      \u0026quot;args\u0026quot;: [\u0026quot;mcp\u0026quot;]\n    }\n  }\n}\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e{\n  \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"mcpServers\"\u003c/span\u003e: {\n    \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"qmd\"\u003c/span\u003e: {\n      \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"command\"\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003eqmd\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e,\n      \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"args\"\u003c/span\u003e: [\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003emcp\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e]\n    }\n  }\n}\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eClaude Code\u003c/strong\u003e — Install the plugin (recommended):\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"claude plugin marketplace add tobi/qmd\nclaude plugin install qmd@qmd\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003eclaude plugin marketplace add tobi/qmd\nclaude plugin install qmd@qmd\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eOr configure MCP manually in \u003ccode\u003e~/.claude/settings.json\u003c/code\u003e:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-json notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"{\n  \u0026quot;mcpServers\u0026quot;: {\n    \u0026quot;qmd\u0026quot;: {\n      \u0026quot;command\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;qmd\u0026quot;,\n      \u0026quot;args\u0026quot;: [\u0026quot;mcp\u0026quot;]\n    }\n  }\n}\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e{\n  \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"mcpServers\"\u003c/span\u003e: {\n    \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"qmd\"\u003c/span\u003e: {\n      \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"command\"\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003eqmd\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e,\n      \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"args\"\u003c/span\u003e: [\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003emcp\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e]\n    }\n  }\n}\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch4 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eHTTP Transport\u003c/h4\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-http-transport\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: HTTP Transport\" href=\"#http-transport\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eBy default, QMD's MCP server uses stdio (launched as a subprocess by each client). For a shared, long-lived server that avoids repeated model loading, use the HTTP transport:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"# Foreground (Ctrl-C to stop)\nqmd mcp --http                    # localhost:8181\nqmd mcp --http --port 8080        # custom port\n\n# Background daemon\nqmd mcp --http --daemon           # start, writes PID to ~/.cache/qmd/mcp.pid\nqmd mcp stop                      # stop via PID file\nqmd status                        # shows \u0026quot;MCP: running (PID ...)\u0026quot; when active\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Foreground (Ctrl-C to stop)\u003c/span\u003e\nqmd mcp --http                    \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e localhost:8181\u003c/span\u003e\nqmd mcp --http --port 8080        \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e custom port\u003c/span\u003e\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Background daemon\u003c/span\u003e\nqmd mcp --http --daemon           \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e start, writes PID to ~/.cache/qmd/mcp.pid\u003c/span\u003e\nqmd mcp stop                      \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e stop via PID file\u003c/span\u003e\nqmd status                        \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e shows \"MCP: running (PID ...)\" when active\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe HTTP server exposes two endpoints:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003ePOST /mcp\u003c/code\u003e — MCP Streamable HTTP (JSON responses, stateless)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003eGET /health\u003c/code\u003e — liveness check with uptime\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eLLM models stay loaded in VRAM across requests. Embedding/reranking contexts are disposed after 5 min idle and transparently recreated on the next request (~1s penalty, models remain loaded).\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003ePoint any MCP client at \u003ccode\u003ehttp://localhost:8181/mcp\u003c/code\u003e to connect.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eSDK / Library Usage\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-sdk--library-usage\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: SDK / Library Usage\" href=\"#sdk--library-usage\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 pre-expanded structured queries:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-ts notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"// Simple query — auto-expanded via LLM, then BM25 + vector + reranking\nconst results = await store.search({ query: \u0026quot;authentication flow\u0026quot; })\n\n// With options\nconst results2 = await store.search({\n  query: \u0026quot;rate limiting\u0026quot;,\n  intent: \u0026quot;API throttling and abuse prevention\u0026quot;,\n  collection: \u0026quot;docs\u0026quot;,\n  limit: 5,\n  minScore: 0.3,\n  explain: true,\n})\n\n// Pre-expanded queries — skip auto-expansion, control each sub-query\nconst results3 = await store.search({\n  queries: [\n    { type: 'lex', query: '\u0026quot;connection pool\u0026quot; timeout -redis' },\n    { type: 'vec', query: 'why do database connections time out under load' },\n  ],\n  collections: [\u0026quot;docs\u0026quot;, \u0026quot;notes\u0026quot;],\n})\n\n// Skip reranking for faster results\nconst fast = await store.search({ query: \u0026quot;auth\u0026quot;, rerank: false })\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe \u003ccode\u003equery\u003c/code\u003e command uses \u003cstrong\u003eReciprocal Rank Fusion (RRF)\u003c/strong\u003e with position-aware blending:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003col dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eQuery Expansion\u003c/strong\u003e: Original query (×2 for weighting) + 1 LLM variation\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eParallel Retrieval\u003c/strong\u003e: Each query searches both FTS and vector indexes\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eRRF Fusion\u003c/strong\u003e: Combine all result lists using \u003ccode\u003escore = Σ(1/(k+rank+1))\u003c/code\u003e where k=60\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eTop-Rank Bonus\u003c/strong\u003e: Documents ranking #1 in any list get +0.05, #2-3 get +0.02\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eTop-K Selection\u003c/strong\u003e: Take top 30 candidates for reranking\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eRe-ranking\u003c/strong\u003e: LLM scores each document (yes/no with logprobs confidence)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003ePosition-Aware Blending\u003c/strong\u003e:\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eRRF rank 1-3: 75% retrieval, 25% reranker (preserves exact matches)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eRRF rank 4-10: 60% retrieval, 40% reranker\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eRRF rank 11+: 40% retrieval, 60% reranker (trust reranker more)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eWhy this approach\u003c/strong\u003e: Pure RRF can dilute exact matches when expanded queries don't match. The top-rank bonus preserves documents that score #1 for the original query. Position-aware blending prevents the reranker from destroying high-confidence retrieval results.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" use):\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cmarkdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\u003ctable\u003e\n\u003cthead\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eModel\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003ePurpose\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eSize\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/thead\u003e\n\u003ctbody\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eembeddinggemma-300M-Q8_0\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eVector embeddings (default)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e~300MB\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eqwen3-reranker-0.6b-q8_0\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eRe-ranking\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e~640MB\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eqmd-query-expansion-1.7B-q4_k_m\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eQuery expansion (fine-tuned)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e~1.1GB\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/tbody\u003e\n\u003c/table\u003e\u003c/markdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eModels are downloaded from HuggingFace and cached in \u003ccode\u003e~/.cache/qmd/models/\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" environment variable.\nThis is useful for multilingual corpora (e.g. Chinese, Japanese, Korean) where\n\u003ccode\u003eembeddinggemma-300M\u003c/code\u003e has limited coverage.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"# Use Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B for better multilingual (CJK) support\nexport QMD_EMBED_MODEL=\u0026quot;hf:Qwen/Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B-GGUF/Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B-Q8_0.gguf\u0026quot;\n\n# After changing the model, re-embed all collections:\nqmd embed -f\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Use Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B for better multilingual (CJK) support\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eexport\u003c/span\u003e QMD_EMBED_MODEL=\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003ehf:Qwen/Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B-GGUF/Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B-Q8_0.gguf\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e After changing the model, re-embed all collections:\u003c/span\u003e\nqmd embed -f\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eSupported model families:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eembeddinggemma\u003c/strong\u003e (default) — English-optimized, small footprint\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eQwen3-Embedding\u003c/strong\u003e — Multilingual (119 languages including CJK), MTEB top-ranked\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cblockquote\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eNote:\u003c/strong\u003e When switching embedding models, you must re-index with \u003ccode\u003eqmd embed -f\u003c/code\u003e\nsince vectors are not cross-compatible between models. The prompt format is\nautomatically adjusted for each model family.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/blockquote\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"# Create a collection from current directory\nqmd collection add . --name myproject\n\n# Create a collection with explicit path and custom glob mask\nqmd collection add ~/Documents/notes --name notes --mask \u0026quot;**/*.md\u0026quot;\n\n# List all collections\nqmd collection list\n\n# Remove a collection\nqmd collection remove myproject\n\n# Rename a collection\nqmd collection rename myproject my-project\n\n# List files in a collection\nqmd ls notes\nqmd ls notes/subfolder\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Create a collection from current directory\u003c/span\u003e\nqmd collection add \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e.\u003c/span\u003e --name myproject\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Create a collection with explicit path and custom glob mask\u003c/span\u003e\nqmd collection add \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e~\u003c/span\u003e/Documents/notes --name notes --mask \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e**/*.md\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e List all collections\u003c/span\u003e\nqmd collection list\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Remove a collection\u003c/span\u003e\nqmd collection remove myproject\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Rename a collection\u003c/span\u003e\nqmd collection rename myproject my-project\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e List files in a collection\u003c/span\u003e\nqmd ls notes\nqmd ls notes/subfolder\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"# Embed all indexed documents (900 tokens/chunk, 15% overlap)\nqmd embed\n\n# Force re-embed everything\nqmd embed -f\n\n# Enable AST-aware chunking for code files (TS, JS, Python, Go, Rust)\nqmd embed --chunk-strategy auto\n\n# Also works with query for consistent chunk selection\nqmd query \u0026quot;auth flow\u0026quot; --chunk-strategy auto\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Embed all indexed documents (900 tokens/chunk, 15% overlap)\u003c/span\u003e\nqmd embed\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Force re-embed everything\u003c/span\u003e\nqmd embed -f\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Enable AST-aware chunking for code files (TS, JS, Python, Go, Rust)\u003c/span\u003e\nqmd embed --chunk-strategy auto\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Also works with query for consistent chunk selection\u003c/span\u003e\nqmd query \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003eauth flow\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e --chunk-strategy auto\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eAST-aware chunking\u003c/strong\u003e (\u003ccode\u003e--chunk-strategy auto\u003c/code\u003e) uses tree-sitter to chunk code\nfiles at function, class, and import boundaries instead of arbitrary text\npositions. This produces higher-quality chunks and better search results for\ncodebases. Markdown and other file types always use regex-based chunking\nregardless of strategy.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe default is \u003ccode\u003eregex\u003c/code\u003e (existing behavior). Use \u003ccode\u003e--chunk-strategy auto\u003c/code\u003e to\nopt in. Run \u003ccode\u003eqmd status\u003c/code\u003e to verify which grammars are available.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cblockquote\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eNote:\u003c/strong\u003e Tree-sitter grammars are optional dependencies. If they are not\ninstalled, \u003ccode\u003e--chunk-strategy auto\u003c/code\u003e falls back to regex-only chunking\nautomatically. Tested on both Node.js and Bun.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/blockquote\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" content.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"# Add context to a collection (using qmd:// virtual paths)\nqmd context add qmd://notes \u0026quot;Personal notes and ideas\u0026quot;\nqmd context add qmd://docs/api \u0026quot;API documentation\u0026quot;\n\n# Add context from within a collection directory\ncd ~/notes \u0026amp;\u0026amp; qmd context add \u0026quot;Personal notes and ideas\u0026quot;\ncd ~/notes/work \u0026amp;\u0026amp; qmd context add \u0026quot;Work-related notes\u0026quot;\n\n# Add global context (applies to all collections)\nqmd context add / \u0026quot;Knowledge base for my projects\u0026quot;\n\n# List all contexts\nqmd context list\n\n# Remove context\nqmd context rm qmd://notes/old\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Add context to a collection (using qmd:// virtual paths)\u003c/span\u003e\nqmd context add qmd://notes \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003ePersonal notes and ideas\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\nqmd context add qmd://docs/api \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003eAPI documentation\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Add context from within a data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                        Search Modes                              │\n├──────────┬───────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤\n│ search   │ BM25 full-text search only                           │\n│ vsearch  │ Vector semantic search only                          │\n│ query    │ Hybrid: FTS + Vector + Query Expansion + Re-ranking  │\n└──────────┴───────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\"\u003e\u003cpre class=\"notranslate\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003e┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                        Search Modes                              │\n├──────────┬───────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤\n│ search   │ BM25 full-text search only                           │\n│ vsearch  │ Vector semantic search only                          │\n│ query    │ Hybrid: FTS + Vector + Query Expansion + Re-ranking  │\n└──────────┴───────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"# Full-text search (fast, keyword-based)\nqmd search \u0026quot;authentication flow\u0026quot;\n\n# Vector search (semantic similarity)\nqmd vsearch \u0026quot;how to login\u0026quot;\n\n# Hybrid search with re-ranking (best quality)\nqmd query \u0026quot;user authentication\u0026quot;\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Full-text search (fast, keyword-based)\u003c/span\u003e\nqmd search \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003eauthentication flow\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Vector search (semantic similarity)\u003c/span\u003e\nqmd vsearch \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan (default: 5, or 20 for --files/--json)\n-c, --collection   # Restrict search to a specific collection\n--all              # Return all matches (use with --min-score to filter)\n--min-score \u0026lt;num\u0026gt;  # Minimum score threshold (default: 0)\n--full             # Show full document content\n--line-numbers     # Add line numbers to output\n--explain          # Include retrieval score traces (query, JSON/CLI output)\n--index \u0026lt;name\u0026gt;     # Use named index\n\n# Output formats (for search and multi-get)\n--files            # Output: docid,score,filepath,context\n--json             # JSON output with snippets\n--csv              # CSV output\n--md               # Markdown output\n--xml              # XML output\n\n# Get options\nqmd get \u0026lt;file\u0026gt;[:line]  # Get document, optionally starting at line\n-l \u0026lt;num\u0026gt;               # Maximum lines to return\n--from \u0026lt;num\u0026gt;           # Start from line number\n\n# Multi-get options\n-l \u0026lt;num\u0026gt;           # Maximum lines per file\n--max-bytes \u0026lt;num\u0026gt;  # Skip files larger than N bytes (default: data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"# Show index status and collections with contexts\nqmd status\n\n# Re-index all collections\nqmd update\n\n# Re-index with git pull first (for remote repos)\nqmd update --pull\n\n# Get document by filepath (with fuzzy matching suggestions)\nqmd get notes/meeting.md\n\n# Get document by docid (from search results)\nqmd get \u0026quot;#abc123\u0026quot;\n\n# Get document starting at line 50, max 100 lines\nqmd get notes/meeting.md:50 -l 100\n\n# Get multiple documents by glob pattern\nqmd multi-get \u0026quot;journals/2025-05*.md\u0026quot;\n\n# Get multiple documents by comma-separated list (supports docids)\nqmd multi-get \u0026quot;doc1.md, doc2.md, #abc123\u0026quot;\n\n# Limit multi-get to files under 20KB\nqmd multi-get \u0026quot;docs/*.md\u0026quot; --max-bytes 20480\n\n# Output multi-get as JSON for agent processing\nqmd multi-get \u0026quot;docs/*.md\u0026quot; --json\n\n# Clean up cache and orphaned data\nqmd llama.cpp GPU backend (\u003ccode\u003emetal\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003evulkan\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003ecuda\u003c/code\u003e) or disable GPU with \u003ccode\u003efalse\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eQMD_EMBED_PARALLELISM\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eautomatic\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eOverride embedding/reranking context parallelism (1-8). Windows CUDA defaults to \u003ccode\u003e1\u003c/code\u003e because parallel CUDA contexts can crash with \u003ccode\u003eggml-cuda.cu:98\u003c/code\u003e; use Vulkan or raise this only if your driver text\"        embedBatch()\n                │\n                └─► Chunks stored with:\n                    - hash: document hash\n                    - seq: chunk sequence (0, 1, 2...)\n markdown break points. This keeps semantic units (sections, paragraphs, code blocks) together.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eBreak Point Scores:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cmarkdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\u003ctable\u003e\n\u003cthead\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003cth\u003ePattern\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eScore\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eDescription\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/thead\u003e\n\u003ctbody\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e# Heading\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e100\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eH1 - major section\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e## Heading\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e90\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eH2 - subsection\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e### Heading\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e80\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eH3\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e#### Heading\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e70\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eH4\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e##### Heading\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e60\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eH5\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e###### Heading\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e50\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eH6\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e```\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e80\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eCode block boundary\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e---\u003c/code\u003e / \u003ccode\u003e***\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e60\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eHorizontal rule\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eBlank line\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e20\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eParagraph boundary\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e- item\u003c/code\u003e / \u003ccode\u003e1. item\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e5\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eList item\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eLine break\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e1\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eMinimal break\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/tbody\u003e\n\u003c/table\u003e\u003c/markdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eAlgorithm:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003col dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eScan document for all break points with scores\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eWhen approaching the 900-token target, search a 200-token window before the cutoff\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eScore each break point: \u003ccode\u003efinalScore = baseScore × (1 - (distance/window)² × 0.7)\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eCut at the highest-scoring break point\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe squared distance decay means a heading 200 tokens back (score ~30) still beats a simple line break at the target (score 1), but a closer heading wins over a distant one.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eCode Fence Protection:\u003c/strong\u003e Break points inside code blocks are ignored—code stays together. If a code block exceeds the chunk size, it's kept whole when possible.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eAST-Aware Chunking (Code Files):\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eFor supported code files, QMD also parses the source with \u003ca href=\"https://tree-sitter.github.io/\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003etree-sitter\u003c/a\u003e and adds AST-derived break points that are merged with the regex scores above:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cmarkdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\u003ctable\u003e\n\u003cthead\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eAST Node\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eScore\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eLanguages\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/thead\u003e\n\u003ctbody\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eClass / interface / struct / impl / trait\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e100\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eAll\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eFunction / method\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e90\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eAll\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eType alias / enum\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e80\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eAll\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eImport / use declaration\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e60\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eAll\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/tbody\u003e\n\u003c/table\u003e\u003c/markdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eSupported for \u003ccode\u003e.ts\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003e.tsx\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003e.js\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003e.jsx\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003e.py\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003e.go\u003c/code\u003e, and \u003ccode\u003e.rs\u003c/code\u003e files. Enable with \u003ccode\u003e--chunk-strategy auto\u003c/code\u003e. Markdown and other file types always use regex chunking.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\"
