DirectoryRichtextContent-module__SharedMarkdownContent__hHXUL" data-hpc="true"><article class="markdown-body entry-content container-lg" itemprop="text"><div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">QMD - Query Markup Documents</h1><a id="user-content-qmd---query-markup-documents" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: QMD - Query Markup Documents" href="#qmd---query-markup-documents"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">An on-device search engine for everything you need to remember. Index your markdown notes, meeting transcripts, documentation, and knowledge bases. Search with keywords or natural language. Ideal for your agentic flows.</p>
<p dir="auto">QMD combines BM25 full-text search, vector semantic search, and LLM re-ranking—all running locally via node-llama-cpp with GGUF models.</p>
<p dir="auto"><a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="/tobi/qmd/blob/main/assets/qmd-architecture.png"><img src="/tobi/qmd/raw/main/assets/qmd-architecture.png" alt="QMD Architecture" style="max-width: 100%;"></a></p>
<p dir="auto">You can read more about QMD's progress in the <a href="/tobi/qmd/blob/main/CHANGELOG.md">CHANGELOG</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Quick Start</h2><a id="user-content-quick-start" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Quick Start" href="#quick-start"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Install globally (Node or Bun)
npm install -g @tobilu/qmd
# or
bun install -g @tobilu/qmd

# Or run directly
npx @tobilu/qmd ...
bunx @tobilu/qmd ...

# Create collections for your notes, docs, and meeting transcripts
qmd collection add ~/notes --name notes
qmd collection add ~/Documents/meetings --name meetings
qmd collection add ~/work/docs --name docs

# Add context to help with search results, each piece of context will be returned when matching sub documents are returned. This works as a tree. This is the key feature of QMD as it allows LLMs to make much better contextual choices when selecting documents. Don't sleep on it!
qmd context add qmd://notes &quot;Personal notes and ideas&quot;
qmd context add qmd://meetings &quot;Meeting transcripts and notes&quot;
qmd context add qmd://docs &quot;Work documentation&quot;

# Generate embeddings for semantic search
qmd embed

# Search across everything
qmd search &quot;project timeline&quot;           # Fast keyword search
qmd vsearch &quot;how to deploy&quot;             # Semantic search
qmd query &quot;quarterly planning process&quot;  # Hybrid + reranking (best quality)

# Get a specific document
qmd get &quot;meetings/2024-01-15.md&quot;

# Get a document by docid (shown in search results)
qmd get &quot;#abc123&quot;

# Get multiple documents by glob pattern
qmd multi-get &quot;journals/2025-05*.md&quot;

# Search within a specific collection
qmd search &quot;API&quot; -c notes

# Export all matches for an agent
qmd search &quot;API&quot; --all --files --min-score 0.3"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Install globally (Node or Bun)</span>
npm install -g @tobilu/qmd
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> or</span>
bun install -g @tobilu/qmd

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Or run directly</span>
npx @tobilu/qmd ...
bunx @tobilu/qmd ...

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Create collections for your notes, docs, and meeting transcripts</span>
qmd collection add <span class="pl-k">~</span>/notes --name notes
qmd collection add <span class="pl-k">~</span>/Documents/meetings --name meetings
qmd collection add <span class="pl-k">~</span>/work/docs --name docs

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Add context to help with search results, each piece of context will be returned when matching sub documents are returned. This works as a tree. This is the key feature of QMD as it allows LLMs to make much better contextual choices when selecting documents. Don't sleep on it!</span>
qmd context add qmd://notes <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>Personal notes and ideas<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
qmd context add qmd://meetings <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>Meeting transcripts and notes<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
qmd context add qmd://docs <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>Work documentation<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Generate embeddings for semantic search</span>
qmd embed

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Search across everything</span>
qmd search <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>project timeline<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>           <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Fast keyword search</span>
qmd vsearch <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>how to deploy<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>             <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Semantic search</span>
qmd query <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>quarterly planning process<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Hybrid + reranking (best quality)</span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Get a specific document</span>
qmd get <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>meetings/2024-01-15.md<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Get a document by docid (shown in search results)</span>
qmd get <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>#abc123<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Get multiple documents by glob pattern</span>
qmd multi-get <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>journals/2025-05*.md<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Search within a specific collection</span>
qmd search <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>API<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> -c notes

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Export all matches for an agent</span>
qmd search <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>API<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> --all --files --min-score 0.3</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Using with AI Agents</h3><a id="user-content-using-with-ai-agents" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Using with AI Agents" href="#using-with-ai-agents"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">QMD's <code>--json</code> and <code>--files</code> output formats are designed for agentic workflows:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Get structured results for an LLM
qmd search &quot;authentication&quot; --json -n 10

# List all relevant files above a threshold
qmd query &quot;error handling&quot; --all --files --min-score 0.4

# Retrieve full document content
qmd get &quot;docs/api-reference.md&quot; --full"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Get structured results for an LLM</span>
qmd search <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>authentication<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> --json -n 10

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> List all relevant files above a threshold</span>
qmd query <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>error handling<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> --all --files --min-score 0.4

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Retrieve full document content</span>
qmd get <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>docs/api-reference.md<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> --full</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">MCP Server</h3><a id="user-content-mcp-server" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: MCP Server" href="#mcp-server"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Although the tool works perfectly fine when you just tell your agent to use it on the command line, it also exposes an MCP (Model Context Protocol) server for tighter integration.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Tools exposed:</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>query</code> — Search with typed sub-queries (<code>lex</code>/<code>vec</code>/<code>hyde</code>), combined via RRF + reranking</li>
<li><code>get</code> — Retrieve a document by path or docid (with fuzzy matching suggestions)</li>
<li><code>multi_get</code> — Batch retrieve by glob pattern, comma-separated list, or docids</li>
<li><code>status</code> — Index health and collection info</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Claude Desktop configuration</strong> (<code>~/Library/Application Support/Claude/claude_desktop_config.json</code>):</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-json notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="{
  &quot;mcpServers&quot;: {
    &quot;qmd&quot;: {
      &quot;command&quot;: &quot;qmd&quot;,
      &quot;args&quot;: [&quot;mcp&quot;]
    }
  }
}"><pre>{
  <span class="pl-ent">"mcpServers"</span>: {
    <span class="pl-ent">"qmd"</span>: {
      <span class="pl-ent">"command"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>qmd<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>,
      <span class="pl-ent">"args"</span>: [<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>mcp<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>]
    }
  }
}</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Claude Code</strong> — Install the plugin (recommended):</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="claude plugin marketplace add tobi/qmd
claude plugin install qmd@qmd"><pre>claude plugin marketplace add tobi/qmd
claude plugin install qmd@qmd</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Or configure MCP manually in <code>~/.claude/settings.json</code>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-json notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="{
  &quot;mcpServers&quot;: {
    &quot;qmd&quot;: {
      &quot;command&quot;: &quot;qmd&quot;,
      &quot;args&quot;: [&quot;mcp&quot;]
    }
  }
}"><pre>{
  <span class="pl-ent">"mcpServers"</span>: {
    <span class="pl-ent">"qmd"</span>: {
      <span class="pl-ent">"command"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>qmd<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>,
      <span class="pl-ent">"args"</span>: [<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>mcp<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>]
    }
  }
}</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">HTTP Transport</h4><a id="user-content-http-transport" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: HTTP Transport" href="#http-transport"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">By default, QMD's MCP server uses stdio (launched as a subprocess by each client). For a shared, long-lived server that avoids repeated model loading, use the HTTP transport:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Foreground (Ctrl-C to stop)
qmd mcp --http                    # localhost:8181
qmd mcp --http --port 8080        # custom port

# Background daemon
qmd mcp --http --daemon           # start, writes PID to ~/.cache/qmd/mcp.pid
qmd mcp stop                      # stop via PID file
qmd status                        # shows &quot;MCP: running (PID ...)&quot; when active"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Foreground (Ctrl-C to stop)</span>
qmd mcp --http                    <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> localhost:8181</span>
qmd mcp --http --port 8080        <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> custom port</span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Background daemon</span>
qmd mcp --http --daemon           <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> start, writes PID to ~/.cache/qmd/mcp.pid</span>
qmd mcp stop                      <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> stop via PID file</span>
qmd status                        <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> shows "MCP: running (PID ...)" when active</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">The HTTP server exposes two endpoints:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>POST /mcp</code> — MCP Streamable HTTP (JSON responses, stateless)</li>
<li><code>GET /health</code> — liveness check with uptime</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">LLM models stay loaded in VRAM across requests. Embedding/reranking contexts are disposed after 5 min idle and transparently recreated on the next request (~1s penalty, models remain loaded).</p>
<p dir="auto">Point any MCP client at <code>http://localhost:8181/mcp</code> to connect.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">SDK / Library Usage</h3><a id="user-content-sdk--library-usage" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: SDK / Library Usage" href="#sdk--library-usage"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Use QMD as a library in your own Node.js or Bun applications.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Installation</h4><a id="user-content-installation" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Installation" href="#installation"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="npm install @tobilu/qmd"><pre>npm install @tobilu/qmd</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Quick Start</h4><a id="user-content-quick-start-1" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Quick Start" href="#quick-start-1"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-ts notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="import { createStore } from '@tobilu/qmd'

const store = await createStore({
  dbPath: './my-index.sqlite',
  config: {
    collections: {
      docs: { path: '/path/to/docs', pattern: '**/*.md' },
    },
  },
})

const results = await store.search({ query: &quot;authentication flow&quot; })
console.log(results.map(r =&gt; `${r.title} (${Math.round(r.score * 100)}%)`))

await store.close()"><pre><span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span> <span class="pl-s1">createStore</span> <span class="pl-kos">}</span> <span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s">'@tobilu/qmd'</span>

<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">store</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-en">createStore</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">dbPath</span>: <span class="pl-s">'./my-index.sqlite'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">config</span>: <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
    <span class="pl-c1">collections</span>: <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
      <span class="pl-c1">docs</span>: <span class="pl-kos">{</span> <span class="pl-c1">path</span>: <span class="pl-s">'/path/to/docs'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-c1">pattern</span>: <span class="pl-s">'**/*.md'</span> <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
    <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>

<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">results</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-s1">store</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">search</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">{</span> <span class="pl-c1">query</span>: <span class="pl-s">"authentication flow"</span> <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>
<span class="pl-smi">console</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">log</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">results</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">map</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">r</span> <span class="pl-c1">=&gt;</span> <span class="pl-s">`<span class="pl-s1"><span class="pl-kos">${</span><span class="pl-s1">r</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">title</span><span class="pl-kos">}</span></span> (<span class="pl-s1"><span class="pl-kos">${</span><span class="pl-v">Math</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">round</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">r</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">score</span> <span class="pl-c1">*</span> <span class="pl-c1">100</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">}</span></span>%)`</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>

<span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-s1">store</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">close</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Store Creation</h4><a id="user-content-store-creation" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Store Creation" href="#store-creation"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><code>createStore()</code> accepts three modes:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-ts notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="import { createStore } from '@tobilu/qmd'

// 1. Inline config — no files needed besides the DB
const store = await createStore({
  dbPath: './index.sqlite',
  config: {
    collections: {
      docs: { path: '/path/to/docs', pattern: '**/*.md' },
      notes: { path: '/path/to/notes' },
    },
  },
})

// 2. YAML config file — collections defined in a file
const store2 = await createStore({
  dbPath: './index.sqlite',
  configPath: './qmd.yml',
})

// 3. DB-only — reopen a previously configured store
const store3 = await createStore({ dbPath: './index.sqlite' })"><pre><span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span> <span class="pl-s1">createStore</span> <span class="pl-kos">}</span> <span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s">'@tobilu/qmd'</span>

<span class="pl-c">// 1. Inline config — no files needed besides the DB</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">store</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-en">createStore</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">dbPath</span>: <span class="pl-s">'./index.sqlite'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">config</span>: <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
    <span class="pl-c1">collections</span>: <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
      <span class="pl-c1">docs</span>: <span class="pl-kos">{</span> <span class="pl-c1">path</span>: <span class="pl-s">'/path/to/docs'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-c1">pattern</span>: <span class="pl-s">'**/*.md'</span> <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
      <span class="pl-c1">notes</span>: <span class="pl-kos">{</span> <span class="pl-c1">path</span>: <span class="pl-s">'/path/to/notes'</span> <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
    <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>

<span class="pl-c">// 2. YAML config file — collections defined in a file</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">store2</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-en">createStore</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">dbPath</span>: <span class="pl-s">'./index.sqlite'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">configPath</span>: <span class="pl-s">'./qmd.yml'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>

<span class="pl-c">// 3. DB-only — reopen a previously configured store</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">store3</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-en">createStore</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">{</span> <span class="pl-c1">dbPath</span>: <span class="pl-s">'./index.sqlite'</span> <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Search</h4><a id="user-content-search" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Search" href="#search"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The unified <code>search()</code> method handles both simple queries and pre-expanded structured queries:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-ts notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="// Simple query — auto-expanded via LLM, then BM25 + vector + reranking
const results = await store.search({ query: &quot;authentication flow&quot; })

// With options
const results2 = await store.search({
  query: &quot;rate limiting&quot;,
  intent: &quot;API throttling and abuse prevention&quot;,
  collection: &quot;docs&quot;,
  limit: 5,
  minScore: 0.3,
  explain: true,
})

// Pre-expanded queries — skip auto-expansion, control each sub-query
const results3 = await store.search({
  queries: [
    { type: 'lex', query: '&quot;connection pool&quot; timeout -redis' },
    { type: 'vec', query: 'why do database connections time out under load' },
  ],
  collections: [&quot;docs&quot;, &quot;notes&quot;],
})

// Skip reranking for faster results
const fast = await store.search({ query: &quot;auth&quot;, rerank: false })"><pre><span class="pl-c">// Simple query — auto-expanded via LLM, then BM25 + vector + reranking</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">results</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-s1">store</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">search</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">{</span> <span class="pl-c1">query</span>: <span class="pl-s">"authentication flow"</span> <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>

<span class="pl-c">// With options</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">results2</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-s1">store</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">search</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">query</span>: <span class="pl-s">"rate limiting"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">intent</span>: <span class="pl-s">"API throttling and abuse prevention"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">collection</span>: <span class="pl-s">"docs"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">limit</span>: <span class="pl-c1">5</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">minScore</span>: <span class="pl-c1">0.3</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">explain</span>: <span class="pl-c1">true</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>

<span class="pl-c">// Pre-expanded queries — skip auto-expansion, control each sub-query</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">results3</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-s1">store</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">search</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">queries</span>: <span class="pl-kos">[</span>
    <span class="pl-kos">{</span> <span class="pl-c1">type</span>: <span class="pl-s">'lex'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-c1">query</span>: <span class="pl-s">'"connection pool" timeout -redis'</span> <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
    <span class="pl-kos">{</span> <span class="pl-c1">type</span>: <span class="pl-s">'vec'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-c1">query</span>: <span class="pl-s">'why do database connections time out under load'</span> <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">]</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">collections</span>: <span class="pl-kos">[</span><span class="pl-s">"docs"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s">"notes"</span><span class="pl-kos">]</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>

<span class="pl-c">// Skip reranking for faster results</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">fast</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-s1">store</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">search</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">{</span> <span class="pl-c1">query</span>: <span class="pl-s">"auth"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-c1">rerank</span>: <span class="pl-c1">false</span> <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">For direct backend access:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-ts notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="// BM25 keyword search (fast, no LLM)
const lexResults = await store.searchLex(&quot;auth middleware&quot;, { limit: 10 })

// Vector similarity search (embedding model, no reranking)
const vecResults = await store.searchVector(&quot;how users log in&quot;, { limit: 10 })

// Manual query expansion for full control
const expanded = await store.expandQuery(&quot;auth flow&quot;, { intent: &quot;user login&quot; })
const results4 = await store.search({ queries: expanded })"><pre><span class="pl-c">// BM25 keyword search (fast, no LLM)</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">lexResults</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-s1">store</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">searchLex</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">"auth middleware"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span> <span class="pl-c1">limit</span>: <span class="pl-c1">10</span> <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>

<span class="pl-c">// Vector similarity search (embedding model, no reranking)</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">vecResults</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-s1">store</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">searchVector</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">"how users log in"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span> <span class="pl-c1">limit</span>: <span class="pl-c1">10</span> <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>

<span class="pl-c">// Manual query expansion for full control</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">expanded</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-s1">store</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">expandQuery</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">"auth flow"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span> <span class="pl-c1">intent</span>: <span class="pl-s">"user login"</span> <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">results4</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-s1">store</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">search</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">{</span> <span class="pl-c1">queries</span>: <span class="pl-s1">expanded</span> <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Retrieval</h4><a id="user-content-retrieval" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Retrieval" href="#retrieval"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-ts notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="// Get a document by path or docid
const doc = await store.get(&quot;docs/readme.md&quot;)
const byId = await store.get(&quot;#abc123&quot;)

if (!(&quot;error&quot; in doc)) {
  console.log(doc.title, doc.displayPath, doc.context)
}

// Get document body with line range
const body = await store.getDocumentBody(&quot;docs/readme.md&quot;, {
  fromLine: 50,
  maxLines: 100,
})

// Batch retrieve by glob or comma-separated list
const { docs, errors } = await store.multiGet(&quot;docs/**/*.md&quot;, {
  maxBytes: 20480,
})"><pre><span class="pl-c">// Get a document by path or docid</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">doc</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-s1">store</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">get</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">"docs/readme.md"</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">byId</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-s1">store</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">get</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">"#abc123"</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>

<span class="pl-k">if</span> <span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-c1">!</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">"error"</span> <span class="pl-k">in</span> <span class="pl-s1">doc</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-smi">console</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">log</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">doc</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">title</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s1">doc</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">displayPath</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s1">doc</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">context</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span>

<span class="pl-c">// Get document body with line range</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">body</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-s1">store</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">getDocumentBody</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">"docs/readme.md"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">fromLine</span>: <span class="pl-c1">50</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">maxLines</span>: <span class="pl-c1">100</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>

<span class="pl-c">// Batch retrieve by glob or comma-separated list</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span> docs<span class="pl-kos">,</span> errors <span class="pl-kos">}</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-s1">store</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">multiGet</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">"docs/**/*.md"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">maxBytes</span>: <span class="pl-c1">20480</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Collections</h4><a id="user-content-collections" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Collections" href="#collections"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-ts notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="// Add a collection
await store.addCollection(&quot;myapp&quot;, {
  path: &quot;/src/myapp&quot;,
  pattern: &quot;**/*.ts&quot;,
  ignore: [&quot;node_modules/**&quot;, &quot;*.test.ts&quot;],
})

// List collections with document stats
const collections = await store.listCollections()
// =&gt; [{ name, pwd, glob_pattern, doc_count, active_count, last_modified, includeByDefault }]

// Get names of collections included in queries by default
const defaults = await store.getDefaultCollectionNames()

// Remove / rename
await store.removeCollection(&quot;myapp&quot;)
await store.renameCollection(&quot;old-name&quot;, &quot;new-name&quot;)"><pre><span class="pl-c">// Add a collection</span>
<span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-s1">store</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">addCollection</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">"myapp"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">path</span>: <span class="pl-s">"/src/myapp"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">pattern</span>: <span class="pl-s">"**/*.ts"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">ignore</span>: <span class="pl-kos">[</span><span class="pl-s">"node_modules/**"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s">"*.test.ts"</span><span class="pl-kos">]</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>

<span class="pl-c">// List collections with document stats</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">collections</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-s1">store</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">listCollections</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>
<span class="pl-c">// =&gt; [{ name, pwd, glob_pattern, doc_count, active_count, last_modified, includeByDefault }]</span>

<span class="pl-c">// Get names of collections included in queries by default</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">defaults</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-s1">store</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">getDefaultCollectionNames</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>

<span class="pl-c">// Remove / rename</span>
<span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-s1">store</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">removeCollection</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">"myapp"</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>
<span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-s1">store</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">renameCollection</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">"old-name"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s">"new-name"</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Context</h4><a id="user-content-context" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Context" href="#context"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Context adds descriptive metadata that improves search relevance and is returned alongside results:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-ts notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="// Add context for a path within a collection
await store.addContext(&quot;docs&quot;, &quot;/api&quot;, &quot;REST API reference documentation&quot;)

// Set global context (applies to all collections)
await store.setGlobalContext(&quot;Internal engineering documentation&quot;)

// List all contexts
const contexts = await store.listContexts()
// =&gt; [{ collection, path, context }]

// Remove context
await store.removeContext(&quot;docs&quot;, &quot;/api&quot;)
await store.setGlobalContext(undefined)  // clear global"><pre><span class="pl-c">// Add context for a path within a collection</span>
<span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-s1">store</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">addContext</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">"docs"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s">"/api"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s">"REST API reference documentation"</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>

<span class="pl-c">// Set global context (applies to all collections)</span>
<span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-s1">store</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">setGlobalContext</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">"Internal engineering documentation"</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>

<span class="pl-c">// List all contexts</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">contexts</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-s1">store</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">listContexts</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>
<span class="pl-c">// =&gt; [{ collection, path, context }]</span>

<span class="pl-c">// Remove context</span>
<span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-s1">store</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">removeContext</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">"docs"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s">"/api"</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>
<span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-s1">store</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">setGlobalContext</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-c1">undefined</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>  <span class="pl-c">// clear global</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Indexing</h4><a id="user-content-indexing" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Indexing" href="#indexing"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-ts notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="// Re-index collections by scanning the filesystem
const result = await store.update({
  collections: [&quot;docs&quot;],  // optional — defaults to all
  onProgress: ({ collection, file, current, total }) =&gt; {
    console.log(`[${collection}] ${current}/${total} ${file}`)
  },
})
// =&gt; { collections, indexed, updated, unchanged, removed, needsEmbedding }

// Generate vector embeddings
const embedResult = await store.embed({
  force: false,           // true to re-embed everything
  chunkStrategy: &quot;auto&quot;,  // &quot;regex&quot; (default) or &quot;auto&quot; (AST for code files)
  onProgress: ({ current, total, collection }) =&gt; {
    console.log(`Embedding ${current}/${total}`)
  },
})"><pre><span class="pl-c">// Re-index collections by scanning the filesystem</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">result</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-s1">store</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">update</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">collections</span>: <span class="pl-kos">[</span><span class="pl-s">"docs"</span><span class="pl-kos">]</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>  <span class="pl-c">// optional — defaults to all</span>
  <span class="pl-en">onProgress</span>: <span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">{</span> collection<span class="pl-kos">,</span> file<span class="pl-kos">,</span> current<span class="pl-kos">,</span> total <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-c1">=&gt;</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
    <span class="pl-smi">console</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">log</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">`[<span class="pl-s1"><span class="pl-kos">${</span><span class="pl-s1">collection</span><span class="pl-kos">}</span></span>] <span class="pl-s1"><span class="pl-kos">${</span><span class="pl-s1">current</span><span class="pl-kos">}</span></span>/<span class="pl-s1"><span class="pl-kos">${</span><span class="pl-s1">total</span><span class="pl-kos">}</span></span> <span class="pl-s1"><span class="pl-kos">${</span><span class="pl-s1">file</span><span class="pl-kos">}</span></span>`</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>
<span class="pl-c">// =&gt; { collections, indexed, updated, unchanged, removed, needsEmbedding }</span>

<span class="pl-c">// Generate vector embeddings</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">embedResult</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-s1">store</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">embed</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">force</span>: <span class="pl-c1">false</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>           <span class="pl-c">// true to re-embed everything</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">chunkStrategy</span>: <span class="pl-s">"auto"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>  <span class="pl-c">// "regex" (default) or "auto" (AST for code files)</span>
  <span class="pl-en">onProgress</span>: <span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">{</span> current<span class="pl-kos">,</span> total<span class="pl-kos">,</span> collection <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-c1">=&gt;</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
    <span class="pl-smi">console</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">log</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">`Embedding <span class="pl-s1"><span class="pl-kos">${</span><span class="pl-s1">current</span><span class="pl-kos">}</span></span>/<span class="pl-s1"><span class="pl-kos">${</span><span class="pl-s1">total</span><span class="pl-kos">}</span></span>`</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Types</h4><a id="user-content-types" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Types" href="#types"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Key types exported for SDK consumers:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-ts notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="import type {
  QMDStore,            // The store interface
  SearchOptions,       // Options for search()
  LexSearchOptions,    // Options for searchLex()
  VectorSearchOptions, // Options for searchVector()
  HybridQueryResult,   // Search result with score, snippet, context
  SearchResult,        // Result from searchLex/searchVector
  ExpandedQuery,       // Typed sub-query { type: 'lex'|'vec'|'hyde', query }
  DocumentResult,      // Document metadata + body
  DocumentNotFound,    // Error with similarFiles suggestions
  MultiGetResult,      // Batch retrieval result
  UpdateProgress,      // Progress callback info for update()
  UpdateResult,        // Aggregated update result
  EmbedProgress,       // Progress callback info for embed()
  EmbedResult,         // Embedding result
  StoreOptions,        // createStore() options
  CollectionConfig,    // Inline config shape
  IndexStatus,         // From getStatus()
  IndexHealthInfo,     // From getIndexHealth()
} from '@tobilu/qmd'"><pre><span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-k">type</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-v">QMDStore</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>            <span class="pl-c">// The store interface</span>
  <span class="pl-v">SearchOptions</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>       <span class="pl-c">// Options for search()</span>
  <span class="pl-v">LexSearchOptions</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>    <span class="pl-c">// Options for searchLex()</span>
  <span class="pl-v">VectorSearchOptions</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-c">// Options for searchVector()</span>
  <span class="pl-v">HybridQueryResult</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>   <span class="pl-c">// Search result with score, snippet, context</span>
  <span class="pl-v">SearchResult</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>        <span class="pl-c">// Result from searchLex/searchVector</span>
  <span class="pl-v">ExpandedQuery</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>       <span class="pl-c">// Typed sub-query { type: 'lex'|'vec'|'hyde', query }</span>
  <span class="pl-v">DocumentResult</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>      <span class="pl-c">// Document metadata + body</span>
  <span class="pl-v">DocumentNotFound</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>    <span class="pl-c">// Error with similarFiles suggestions</span>
  <span class="pl-v">MultiGetResult</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>      <span class="pl-c">// Batch retrieval result</span>
  <span class="pl-v">UpdateProgress</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>      <span class="pl-c">// Progress callback info for update()</span>
  <span class="pl-v">UpdateResult</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>        <span class="pl-c">// Aggregated update result</span>
  <span class="pl-v">EmbedProgress</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>       <span class="pl-c">// Progress callback info for embed()</span>
  <span class="pl-v">EmbedResult</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>         <span class="pl-c">// Embedding result</span>
  <span class="pl-v">StoreOptions</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>        <span class="pl-c">// createStore() options</span>
  <span class="pl-v">CollectionConfig</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>    <span class="pl-c">// Inline config shape</span>
  <span class="pl-v">IndexStatus</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>         <span class="pl-c">// From getStatus()</span>
  <span class="pl-v">IndexHealthInfo</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>     <span class="pl-c">// From getIndexHealth()</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span> <span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s">'@tobilu/qmd'</span></pre></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Lifecycle</h4><a id="user-content-lifecycle" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Lifecycle" href="#lifecycle"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

await store.close()"><pre><span class="pl-c">// Close the store — disposes LLM models and DB connection</span>
<span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-s1">store</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">close</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">The SDK requires explicit <code>dbPath</code> — no defaults are assumed. This makes it safe to embed in any application without side effects.</p>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Score Normalization &amp; Fusion</h2><a id="user-content-score-normalization--fusion" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Score Normalization &amp; Fusion" href="#score-normalization--fusion"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Fusion Strategy</h3><a id="user-content-fusion-strategy" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Fusion Strategy" href="#fusion-strategy"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The <code>query</code> command uses <strong>Reciprocal Rank Fusion (RRF)</strong> with position-aware blending:</p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li><strong>Query Expansion</strong>: Original query (×2 for weighting) + 1 LLM variation</li>
<li><strong>Parallel Retrieval</strong>: Each query searches both FTS and vector indexes</li>
<li><strong>RRF Fusion</strong>: Combine all result lists using <code>score = Σ(1/(k+rank+1))</code> where k=60</li>
<li><strong>Top-Rank Bonus</strong>: Documents ranking #1 in any list get +0.05, #2-3 get +0.02</li>
<li><strong>Top-K Selection</strong>: Take top 30 candidates for reranking</li>
<li><strong>Re-ranking</strong>: LLM scores each document (yes/no with logprobs confidence)</li>
<li><strong>Position-Aware Blending</strong>:
<ul dir="auto">
<li>RRF rank 1-3: 75% retrieval, 25% reranker (preserves exact matches)</li>
<li>RRF rank 4-10: 60% retrieval, 40% reranker</li>
<li>RRF rank 11+: 40% retrieval, 60% reranker (trust reranker more)</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ol>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Why this approach</strong>: Pure RRF can dilute exact matches when expanded queries don't match. The top-rank bonus preserves documents that score #1 for the original query. Position-aware blending prevents the reranker from destroying high-confidence retrieval results.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Score Interpretation</h3><a id="user-content-score-interpretation" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Score Interpretation" href="#score-interpretation"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Requirements</h2><a id="user-content-requirements" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Requirements" href="#requirements"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">System Requirements</h3><a id="user-content-system-requirements" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: System Requirements" href="#system-requirements"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Node.js</strong> &gt;= 22</li>
<li><strong>Bun</strong> &gt;= 1.0.0</li>
<li><strong>macOS</strong>: Homebrew SQLite (for extension support)
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="brew install sqlite"><pre>brew install sqlite</pre></div>
</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">GGUF Models (via node-llama-cpp)</h3><a id="user-content-gguf-models-via-node-llama-cpp" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: GGUF Models (via node-llama-cpp)" href="#gguf-models-via-node-llama-cpp"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">QMD uses three local GGUF models (auto-downloaded on first use):</p>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Model</th>
<th>Purpose</th>
<th>Size</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>embeddinggemma-300M-Q8_0</code></td>
<td>Vector embeddings (default)</td>
<td>~300MB</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>qwen3-reranker-0.6b-q8_0</code></td>
<td>Re-ranking</td>
<td>~640MB</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>qmd-query-expansion-1.7B-q4_k_m</code></td>
<td>Query expansion (fine-tuned)</td>
<td>~1.1GB</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<p dir="auto">Models are downloaded from HuggingFace and cached in <code>~/.cache/qmd/models/</code>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Custom Embedding Model</h3><a id="user-content-custom-embedding-model" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Custom Embedding Model" href="#custom-embedding-model"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Override the default embedding model via the <code>QMD_EMBED_MODEL</code> environment variable.
This is useful for multilingual corpora (e.g. Chinese, Japanese, Korean) where
<code>embeddinggemma-300M</code> has limited coverage.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Use Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B for better multilingual (CJK) support
export QMD_EMBED_MODEL=&quot;hf:Qwen/Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B-GGUF/Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B-Q8_0.gguf&quot;

# After changing the model, re-embed all collections:
qmd embed -f"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Use Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B for better multilingual (CJK) support</span>
<span class="pl-k">export</span> QMD_EMBED_MODEL=<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>hf:Qwen/Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B-GGUF/Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B-Q8_0.gguf<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> After changing the model, re-embed all collections:</span>
qmd embed -f</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Supported model families:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>embeddinggemma</strong> (default) — English-optimized, small footprint</li>
<li><strong>Qwen3-Embedding</strong> — Multilingual (119 languages including CJK), MTEB top-ranked</li>
</ul>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Note:</strong> When switching embedding models, you must re-index with <code>qmd embed -f</code>
since vectors are not cross-compatible between models. The prompt format is
automatically adjusted for each model family.</p>
</blockquote>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Installation</h2><a id="user-content-installation-1" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Installation" href="#installation-1"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
