<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Usage</h2><a id="user-content-usage" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Usage" href="#usage"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Collection Management</h3><a id="user-content-collection-management" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Collection Management" href="#collection-management"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Create a collection from current directory
qmd collection add . --name myproject

# Create a collection with explicit path and custom glob mask
qmd collection add ~/Documents/notes --name notes --mask &quot;**/*.md&quot;

# List all collections
qmd collection list

# Remove a collection
qmd collection remove myproject

# Rename a collection
qmd collection rename myproject my-project

# List files in a collection
qmd ls notes
qmd ls notes/subfolder"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Create a collection from current directory</span>
qmd collection add <span class="pl-c1">.</span> --name myproject

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Create a collection with explicit path and custom glob mask</span>
qmd collection add <span class="pl-k">~</span>/Documents/notes --name notes --mask <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>**/*.md<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> List all collections</span>
qmd collection list

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Remove a collection</span>
qmd collection remove myproject

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Rename a collection</span>
qmd collection rename myproject my-project

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> List files in a collection</span>
qmd ls notes
qmd ls notes/subfolder</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Generate Vector Embeddings</h3><a id="user-content-generate-vector-embeddings" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Generate Vector Embeddings" href="#generate-vector-embeddings"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Embed all indexed documents (900 tokens/chunk, 15% overlap)
qmd embed

# Force re-embed everything
qmd embed -f

# Enable AST-aware chunking for code files (TS, JS, Python, Go, Rust)
qmd embed --chunk-strategy auto

# Also works with query for consistent chunk selection
qmd query &quot;auth flow&quot; --chunk-strategy auto"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Embed all indexed documents (900 tokens/chunk, 15% overlap)</span>
qmd embed

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Force re-embed everything</span>
qmd embed -f

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Enable AST-aware chunking for code files (TS, JS, Python, Go, Rust)</span>
qmd embed --chunk-strategy auto

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Also works with query for consistent chunk selection</span>
qmd query <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>auth flow<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> --chunk-strategy auto</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>AST-aware chunking</strong> (<code>--chunk-strategy auto</code>) uses tree-sitter to chunk code
files at function, class, and import boundaries instead of arbitrary text
positions. This produces higher-quality chunks and better search results for
codebases. Markdown and other file types always use regex-based chunking
regardless of strategy.</p>
<p dir="auto">The default is <code>regex</code> (existing behavior). Use <code>--chunk-strategy auto</code> to
opt in. Run <code>qmd status</code> to verify which grammars are available.</p>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Note:</strong> Tree-sitter grammars are optional dependencies. If they are not
installed, <code>--chunk-strategy auto</code> falls back to regex-only chunking
automatically. Tested on both Node.js and Bun.</p>
</blockquote>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Context Management</h3><a id="user-content-context-management" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Context Management" href="#context-management"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Context adds descriptive metadata to collections and paths, helping search understand your content.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Add context to a collection (using qmd:// virtual paths)
qmd context add qmd://notes &quot;Personal notes and ideas&quot;
qmd context add qmd://docs/api &quot;API documentation&quot;

# Add context from within a collection directory
cd ~/notes &amp;&amp; qmd context add &quot;Personal notes and ideas&quot;
cd ~/notes/work &amp;&amp; qmd context add &quot;Work-related notes&quot;

# Add global context (applies to all collections)
qmd context add / &quot;Knowledge base for my projects&quot;

# List all contexts
qmd context list

# Remove context
qmd context rm qmd://notes/old"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Add context to a collection (using qmd:// virtual paths)</span>
qmd context add qmd://notes <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>Personal notes and ideas<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
qmd context add qmd://docs/api <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>API documentation<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Add context from within a collection directory</span>
<span class="pl-c1">cd</span> <span class="pl-k">~</span>/notes <span class="pl-k">&amp;&amp;</span> qmd context add <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>Personal notes and ideas<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
<span class="pl-c1">cd</span> <span class="pl-k">~</span>/notes/work <span class="pl-k">&amp;&amp;</span> qmd context add <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>Work-related notes<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Add global context (applies to all collections)</span>
qmd context add / <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>Knowledge base for my projects<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> List all contexts</span>
qmd context list

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Remove context</span>
qmd context rm qmd://notes/old</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Search Commands</h3><a id="user-content-search-commands" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Search Commands" href="#search-commands"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                        Search Modes                              │
├──────────┬───────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ search   │ BM25 full-text search only                           │
│ vsearch  │ Vector semantic search only                          │
│ query    │ Hybrid: FTS + Vector + Query Expansion + Re-ranking  │
└──────────┴───────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘"><pre class="notranslate"><code>┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                        Search Modes                              │
├──────────┬───────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ search   │ BM25 full-text search only                           │
│ vsearch  │ Vector semantic search only                          │
│ query    │ Hybrid: FTS + Vector + Query Expansion + Re-ranking  │
└──────────┴───────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
</code></pre></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Full-text search (fast, keyword-based)
qmd search &quot;authentication flow&quot;

# Vector search (semantic similarity)
qmd vsearch &quot;how to login&quot;

# Hybrid search with re-ranking (best quality)
qmd query &quot;user authentication&quot;"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Full-text search (fast, keyword-based)</span>
qmd search <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>authentication flow<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Vector search (semantic similarity)</span>
qmd vsearch <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>how to login<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Hybrid search with re-ranking (best quality)</span>
qmd query <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>user authentication<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Options</h3><a id="user-content-options" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Options" href="#options"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Search options
-n &lt;num&gt;           # Number of results (default: 5, or 20 for --files/--json)
-c, --collection   # Restrict search to a specific collection
--all              # Return all matches (use with --min-score to filter)
--min-score &lt;num&gt;  # Minimum score threshold (default: 0)
--full             # Show full document content
--line-numbers     # Add line numbers to output
--explain          # Include retrieval score traces (query, JSON/CLI output)
--index &lt;name&gt;     # Use named index

# Output formats (for search and multi-get)
--files            # Output: docid,score,filepath,context
--json             # JSON output with snippets
--csv              # CSV output
--md               # Markdown output
--xml              # XML output

# Get options
qmd get &lt;file&gt;[:line]  # Get document, optionally starting at line
-l &lt;num&gt;               # Maximum lines to return
--from &lt;num&gt;           # Start from line number

# Multi-get options
-l &lt;num&gt;           # Maximum lines per file
--max-bytes &lt;num&gt;  # Skip files larger than N bytes (default: 10KB)"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Search options</span>
-n <span class="pl-k">&lt;</span>num<span class="pl-k">&gt;</span>           <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Number of results (default: 5, or 20 for --files/--json)</span>
-c, --collection   <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Restrict search to a specific collection</span>
--all              <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Return all matches (use with --min-score to filter)</span>
--min-score <span class="pl-k">&lt;</span>num<span class="pl-k">&gt;</span>  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Minimum score threshold (default: 0)</span>
--full             <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Show full document content</span>
--line-numbers     <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Add line numbers to output</span>
--explain          <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Include retrieval score traces (query, JSON/CLI output)</span>
--index <span class="pl-k">&lt;</span>name<span class="pl-k">&gt;</span>     <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Use named index</span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Output formats (for search and multi-get)</span>
--files            <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Output: docid,score,filepath,context</span>
--json             <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> JSON output with snippets</span>
--csv              <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> CSV output</span>
--md               <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Markdown output</span>
--xml              <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> XML output</span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Get options</span>
qmd get <span class="pl-k">&lt;</span>file<span class="pl-k">&gt;</span>[:line]  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Get document, optionally starting at line</span>
-l <span class="pl-k">&lt;</span>num<span class="pl-k">&gt;</span>               <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Maximum lines to return</span>
--from <span class="pl-k">&lt;</span>num<span class="pl-k">&gt;</span>           <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Start from line number</span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Multi-get options</span>
-l <span class="pl-k">&lt;</span>num<span class="pl-k">&gt;</span>           <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Maximum lines per file</span>
--max-bytes <span class="pl-k">&lt;</span>num<span class="pl-k">&gt;</span>  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Skip files larger than N bytes (default: 10KB)</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Output Format</h3><a id="user-content-output-format" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Output Format" href="#output-format"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Default output is colorized CLI format (respects <code>NO_COLOR</code> env).</p>
<p dir="auto">When stdout is a TTY, result paths are emitted as clickable terminal hyperlinks (OSC 8). Clicking a path opens the file in your editor using an editor URI template.</p>
<p dir="auto">When stdout is not a TTY (for example piped to another command or redirected to a file), QMD emits plain text paths with no escape sequences.</p>
<p dir="auto">TTY example:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="docs/guide.md:42 #a1b2c3
Title: Software Craftsmanship
Context: Work documentation
Score: 93%

This section covers the **craftsmanship** of building
quality software with attention to detail.
See also: engineering principles


notes/meeting.md:15 #d4e5f6
Title: Q4 Planning
Context: Personal notes and ideas
Score: 67%

Discussion about code quality and craftsmanship
in the development process."><pre class="notranslate"><code>docs/guide.md:42 #a1b2c3
Title: Software Craftsmanship
Context: Work documentation
Score: 93%

This section covers the **craftsmanship** of building
quality software with attention to detail.
See also: engineering principles


notes/meeting.md:15 #d4e5f6
Title: Q4 Planning
Context: Personal notes and ideas
Score: 67%

Discussion about code quality and craftsmanship
in the development process.
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Configure the editor link target with <code>QMD_EDITOR_URI</code> (or <code>editor_uri</code> in config):</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# VS Code (default)
export QMD_EDITOR_URI=&quot;vscode://file/{path}:{line}:{col}&quot;

# Cursor
export QMD_EDITOR_URI=&quot;cursor://file/{path}:{line}:{col}&quot;

# Zed
export QMD_EDITOR_URI=&quot;zed://file/{path}:{line}:{col}&quot;

# Sublime Text
export QMD_EDITOR_URI=&quot;subl://open?url=file://{path}&amp;line={line}&quot;"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> VS Code (default)</span>
<span class="pl-k">export</span> QMD_EDITOR_URI=<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>vscode://file/{path}:{line}:{col}<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Cursor</span>
<span class="pl-k">export</span> QMD_EDITOR_URI=<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>cursor://file/{path}:{line}:{col}<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Zed</span>
<span class="pl-k">export</span> QMD_EDITOR_URI=<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>zed://file/{path}:{line}:{col}<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Sublime Text</span>
<span class="pl-k">export</span> QMD_EDITOR_URI=<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>subl://open?url=file://{path}&amp;line={line}<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Template placeholders:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>
<p dir="auto"><code>{path}</code> absolute filesystem path (URI-encoded)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto"><code>{line}</code> 1-based line number</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto"><code>{col}</code> or <code>{column}</code> 1-based column number</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Path</strong>: Collection-relative path (e.g., <code>docs/guide.md</code>)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Docid</strong>: Short hash identifier (e.g., <code>#a1b2c3</code>) - use with <code>qmd get #a1b2c3</code></p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Title</strong>: Extracted from document (first heading or filename)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Context</strong>: Path context if configured via <code>qmd context add</code></p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Score</strong>: Color-coded (green &gt;70%, yellow &gt;40%, dim otherwise)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Snippet</strong>: Context around match with query terms highlighted</p>
</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Examples</h3><a id="user-content-examples" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Examples" href="#examples"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Get 10 results with minimum score 0.3
qmd query -n 10 --min-score 0.3 &quot;API design patterns&quot;

# Output as markdown for LLM context
qmd search --md --full &quot;error handling&quot;

# JSON output for scripting
qmd query --json &quot;quarterly reports&quot;

# Inspect how each result was scored (RRF + rerank blend)
qmd query --json --explain &quot;quarterly reports&quot;

# Use separate index for different knowledge base
qmd --index work search &quot;quarterly reports&quot;"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Get 10 results with minimum score 0.3</span>
qmd query -n 10 --min-score 0.3 <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>API design patterns<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Output as markdown for LLM context</span>
qmd search --md --full <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>error handling<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> JSON output for scripting</span>
qmd query --json <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>quarterly reports<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Inspect how each result was scored (RRF + rerank blend)</span>
qmd query --json --explain <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>quarterly reports<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Use separate index for different knowledge base</span>
qmd --index work search <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>quarterly reports<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Index Maintenance</h3><a id="user-content-index-maintenance" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Index Maintenance" href="#index-maintenance"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Show index status and collections with contexts
qmd status

# Re-index all collections
qmd update

# Re-index with git pull first (for remote repos)
qmd update --pull

# Get document by filepath (with fuzzy matching suggestions)
qmd get notes/meeting.md

# Get document by docid (from search results)
qmd get &quot;#abc123&quot;

# Get document starting at line 50, max 100 lines
qmd get notes/meeting.md:50 -l 100

# Get multiple documents by glob pattern
qmd multi-get &quot;journals/2025-05*.md&quot;

# Get multiple documents by comma-separated list (supports docids)
qmd multi-get &quot;doc1.md, doc2.md, #abc123&quot;

# Limit multi-get to files under 20KB
qmd multi-get &quot;docs/*.md&quot; --max-bytes 20480

# Output multi-get as JSON for agent processing
qmd multi-get &quot;docs/*.md&quot; --json

# Clean up cache and orphaned data
qmd cleanup"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Show index status and collections with contexts</span>
qmd status

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Re-index all collections</span>
qmd update

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Re-index with git pull first (for remote repos)</span>
qmd update --pull

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Get document by filepath (with fuzzy matching suggestions)</span>
qmd get notes/meeting.md

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Get document by docid (from search results)</span>
qmd get <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>#abc123<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Get document starting at line 50, max 100 lines</span>
qmd get notes/meeting.md:50 -l 100

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Get multiple documents by glob pattern</span>
qmd multi-get <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>journals/2025-05*.md<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Get multiple documents by comma-separated list (supports docids)</span>
qmd multi-get <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>doc1.md, doc2.md, #abc123<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Limit multi-get to files under 20KB</span>
qmd multi-get <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>docs/*.md<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> --max-bytes 20480

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Output multi-get as JSON for agent processing</span>
qmd multi-get <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>docs/*.md<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> --json

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Clean up cache and orphaned data</span>
qmd cleanup</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Data Storage</h2><a id="user-content-data-storage" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Data Storage" href="#data-storage"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Index stored in: <code>~/.cache/qmd/index.sqlite</code></p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Schema</h3><a id="user-content-schema" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Schema" href="#schema"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-sql notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="collections     -- Indexed directories with name and glob patterns
path_contexts   -- Context descriptions by virtual path (qmd://...)
documents       -- Markdown content with metadata and docid (6-char hash)
documents_fts   -- FTS5 full-text index
content_vectors -- Embedding chunks (hash, seq, pos, 900 tokens each)
vectors_vec     -- sqlite-vec vector index (hash_seq key)
llm_cache       -- Cached LLM responses (query expansion, rerank scores)"><pre>collections     <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">--</span> Indexed directories with name and glob patterns</span>
path_contexts   <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">--</span> Context descriptions by virtual path (qmd://...)</span>
documents       <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">--</span> Markdown content with metadata and docid (6-char hash)</span>
documents_fts   <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">--</span> FTS5 full-text index</span>
content_vectors <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">--</span> Embedding chunks (hash, seq, pos, 900 tokens each)</span>
vectors_vec     <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">--</span> sqlite-vec vector index (hash_seq key)</span>
llm_cache       <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">--</span> Cached LLM responses (query expansion, rerank scores)</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Environment Variables</h2><a id="user-content-environment-variables" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Environment Variables" href="#environment-variables"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Variable</th>
<th>Default</th>
<th>Description</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>XDG_CACHE_HOME</code></td>
<td><code>~/.cache</code></td>
<td>Cache directory location</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>QMD_LLAMA_GPU</code></td>
<td><code>auto</code></td>
<td>Force llama.cpp GPU backend (<code>metal</code>, <code>vulkan</code>, <code>cuda</code>) or disable GPU with <code>false</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>QMD_EMBED_PARALLELISM</code></td>
<td>automatic</td>
<td>Override embedding/reranking context parallelism (1-8). Windows CUDA defaults to <code>1</code> because parallel CUDA contexts can crash with <code>ggml-cuda.cu:98</code>; use Vulkan or raise this only if your driver is stable.</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">How It Works</h2><a id="user-content-how-it-works" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: How It Works" href="#how-it-works"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Indexing Flow</h3><a id="user-content-indexing-flow" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Indexing Flow" href="#indexing-flow"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Embedding Flow</h3><a id="user-content-embedding-flow" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Embedding Flow" href="#embedding-flow"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Documents are chunked into ~900-token pieces with 15% overlap using smart boundary detection:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="Document ──► Smart Chunk (~900 tokens) ──► Format each chunk ──► node-llama-cpp ──► Store Vectors
                │                           &quot;title | text&quot;        embedBatch()
                │
                └─► Chunks stored with:
                    - hash: document hash
                    - seq: chunk sequence (0, 1, 2...)
                    - pos: character position in original"><pre class="notranslate"><code>Document ──► Smart Chunk (~900 tokens) ──► Format each chunk ──► node-llama-cpp ──► Store Vectors
                │                           "title | text"        embedBatch()
                │
                └─► Chunks stored with:
                    - hash: document hash
                    - seq: chunk sequence (0, 1, 2...)
                    - pos: character position in original
</code></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Smart Chunking</h3><a id="user-content-smart-chunking" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Smart Chunking" href="#smart-chunking"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Instead of cutting at hard token boundaries, QMD uses a scoring algorithm to find natural markdown break points. This keeps semantic units (sections, paragraphs, code blocks) together.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Break Point Scores:</strong></p>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Pattern</th>
<th>Score</th>
<th>Description</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code># Heading</code></td>
<td>100</td>
<td>H1 - major section</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>## Heading</code></td>
<td>90</td>
<td>H2 - subsection</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>### Heading</code></td>
<td>80</td>
<td>H3</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>#### Heading</code></td>
<td>70</td>
<td>H4</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>##### Heading</code></td>
<td>60</td>
<td>H5</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>###### Heading</code></td>
<td>50</td>
<td>H6</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>```</code></td>
<td>80</td>
<td>Code block boundary</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>---</code> / <code>***</code></td>
<td>60</td>
<td>Horizontal rule</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Blank line</td>
<td>20</td>
<td>Paragraph boundary</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>- item</code> / <code>1. item</code></td>
<td>5</td>
<td>List item</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Line break</td>
<td>1</td>
<td>Minimal break</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Algorithm:</strong></p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li>Scan document for all break points with scores</li>
<li>When approaching the 900-token target, search a 200-token window before the cutoff</li>
<li>Score each break point: <code>finalScore = baseScore × (1 - (distance/window)² × 0.7)</code></li>
<li>Cut at the highest-scoring break point</li>
</ol>
<p dir="auto">The squared distance decay means a heading 200 tokens back (score ~30) still beats a simple line break at the target (score 1), but a closer heading wins over a distant one.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Code Fence Protection:</strong> Break points inside code blocks are ignored—code stays together. If a code block exceeds the chunk size, it's kept whole when possible.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>AST-Aware Chunking (Code Files):</strong></p>
<p dir="auto">For supported code files, QMD also parses the source with <a href="https://tree-sitter.github.io/" rel="nofollow">tree-sitter</a> and adds AST-derived break points that are merged with the regex scores above:</p>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>AST Node</th>
<th>Score</th>
<th>Languages</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td>Class / interface / struct / impl / trait</td>
<td>100</td>
<td>All</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Function / method</td>
<td>90</td>
<td>All</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Type alias / enum</td>
<td>80</td>
<td>All</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Import / use declaration</td>
<td>60</td>
<td>All</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<p dir="auto">Supported for <code>.ts</code>, <code>.tsx</code>, <code>.js</code>, <code>.jsx</code>, <code>.py</code>, <code>.go</code>, and <code>.rs</code> files. Enable with <code>--chunk-strategy auto</code>. Markdown and other file types always use regex chunking.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Query Flow (Hybrid)</h3><a id="user-content-query-flow-hybrid" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Query Flow (Hybrid)" href="#query-flow-hybrid"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Model Configuration</h2><a id="user-content-model-configuration" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Model Configuration" href="#model-configuration"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Models are configured in <code>src/llm.ts</code> as HuggingFace URIs:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-ts notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="const DEFAULT_EMBED_MODEL = &quot;hf:ggml-org/embeddinggemma-300M-GGUF/embeddinggemma-300M-Q8_0.gguf&quot;;
const DEFAULT_RERANK_MODEL = &quot;hf:ggml-org/Qwen3-Reranker-0.6B-Q8_0-GGUF/qwen3-reranker-0.6b-q8_0.gguf&quot;;
const DEFAULT_GENERATE_MODEL = &quot;hf:tobil/qmd-query-expansion-1.7B-gguf/qmd-query-expansion-1.7B-q4_k_m.gguf&quot;;"><pre><span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-c1">DEFAULT_EMBED_MODEL</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s">"hf:ggml-org/embeddinggemma-300M-GGUF/embeddinggemma-300M-Q8_0.gguf"</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-c1">DEFAULT_RERANK_MODEL</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s">"hf:ggml-org/Qwen3-Reranker-0.6B-Q8_0-GGUF/qwen3-reranker-0.6b-q8_0.gguf"</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-c1">DEFAULT_GENERATE_MODEL</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s">"hf:tobil/qmd-query-expansion-1.7B-gguf/qmd-query-expansion-1.7B-q4_k_m.gguf"</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">EmbeddingGemma Prompt Format</h3><a id="user-content-embeddinggemma-prompt-format" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: EmbeddingGemma Prompt Format" href="#embeddinggemma-prompt-format"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

&quot;task: search result | query: {query}&quot;

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Qwen3-Reranker</h3><a id="user-content-qwen3-reranker" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Qwen3-Reranker" href="#qwen3-reranker"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Uses node-llama-cpp's <code>createRankingContext()</code> and <code>rankAndSort()</code> API for cross-encoder reranking. Returns documents sorted by relevance score (0.0 - 1.0).</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Qwen3 (Query Expansion)</h3><a id="user-content-qwen3-query-expansion" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Qwen3 (Query Expansion)" href="#qwen3-query-expansion"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Used for generating query variations via <code>LlamaChatSession</code>.</p>
